Though revenues missed slightly, strong cost management and sales leverage drove profitability. FY2027 guidance provided with expected 4% North America comp sales growth.
DPZ | positive | EPS of $4.60 beat consensus by 6.2% with 12.7% YoY earnings growth. Revenues exceeded expectations with 7.1% supply chain revenue growth and higher franchise royalties. Strong pricing power demonstrated with 2.2% food basket pricing increase offsetting sluggish same-store sales.

The total net proceeds are projected at $2.3 billion. DASH | positive | Third-party delivery platforms like DoorDash have captured customer demand that would have otherwise gone to Pizza Hut, reducing the brand's direct sales and strengthening DoorDash's market position.
PEP | neutral | Historical context only; PepsiCo acquired Pizza Hut in 1977 and spun it off as part of Tricon Global Restaurants (later Yum Brands) in 1997, so this transaction does not directly impact PepsiCo.

<<<SUMMARY>>>
Five consumer stocks—Williams-Sonoma, Domino's Pizza, TJX Companies, Lowe's, and Costco Wholesale—have all more than doubled their dividends since 2019. Each company is backed by specific growth initiatives: Williams-Sonoma's AI shopping assistants, Domino's loyalty program expansion, TJX's off-price retail strength, Lowe's professional contractor market acquisitions, and Costco's strong membership renewal rates. However, some face headwinds like Domino's slower sales and Costco's declining comparable sales growth.
<<<INSIGHTS>>>
WSM | positive | Dividend grew 225% since 2019; AI shopping assistants driving 700% jump in customer engagement and 620% revenue increase; raised full-year guidance.
DPZ | neutral | Dividend tripled since 2019 with strong loyalty program growth (37.3M users), but facing headwinds from slower sales, inflation, and increased competition. Berkshire Hathaway divested its position.
TJX | positive | Dividend grew 146% since 2019; 34 consecutive years of comparable sales growth; off-price retail model benefits from economic uncertainty; raised full-year profit forecast and plans expansion to 7,500 stores.
LOW | positive | Dividend grew 143% since 2019; strategic acquisitions (Foundation Building Materials for $8.8B and Artisan Design Group for $1.325B) to capture $250B professional contractor market.
COST | neutral | Dividend grew 126% since 2019 plus special dividends; fifth consecutive quarterly earnings beat and 92.3% membership renewal rate are positive, but underlying sales growth has slowed for three consecutive months, raising demand concerns.
